# Frozen Markdown source inventory and one-by-one CLI judgments

**Frozen denominator:** 393 tracked repository Markdown paths + 34 supplemental paths = 427. Repository sources pin to `fac71702f179c88990914ccd098e0bd52e7e7131`. The three delta-reviewed current documents below remain the same three repository paths; they do not change the frozen denominator. Exact duplicates are retained as alias rows, while canonical bytes are reviewed once. Empty files and the symlink have explicit dispositions.

Repository links pin the frozen commit. Every supplemental source link opens the [supplied review bundle](https://app.devin.ai/attachments/93cae10f-5664-448d-aecd-b1febfcba3fb/review-bundle.tar.gz); the label names the internal archive path and assigned line range. The committed [canonical Markdown review coverage/method summary](markdown-review-coverage.md) records the range reconciliation and reading-status caveats.

Judgment is derived from the reviewers’ `disposition` fields, not from incidental words in idea prose. The exact per-range disposition is retained below. Mixed actions remain mixed. The 675 range-level document ideas are shown in full on their canonical rows with batch/range provenance; no idea text is truncated. `full_text` is the reviewers’ reported status, not independent proof of their internal reading process.
